# Sandbox manifest: session_01XHH3k24fjmkpmmt28xSaYv

Every file in this session's scratch folder (`/tmp/claude-0/.../scratchpad`) at 2026-10-04 ~08:30 UTC. "committed" files are copied here under the same relative path. Excluded files are listed with the reason and the source or command that regenerates them. The repo checkout itself is in git (branch claude/data-historical at e5552a4, PR #127).

Outside the scratch folder: `/tmp/ac.bak`, `/tmp/ac2.bak` and `/tmp/actest/` are earlier drafts and a test dir of `research/historical/ci/archive-check.sh` (superseded; the final version is in git). Other `/tmp` entries are harness files (diag logs, the system prompt, sockets) and temporary dirs made by test runs (`research/historical/ci/test-ci.sh`, worker tests); they are excluded: not work products, regenerated by running the tests. Subagent transcripts in `tasks/` are excluded (harness logs); their results are in the handover note.
